It makes leaving the user out a stated choice instead of a forgotten field (A's run-as: image, A:internal/spec/runas.go:18-21). The rest of the posture is written either way, as A does today (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:186-194). B never sets it.
- **compose.Service.Restart** `string` (in both, required): `restart: "<policy>"` through Quote, after user. Grammar compose's: no, always, unless-stopped, on-failure or on-failure:N (A:internal/validate/validate.go:1672). Quoted because a bare `no` is a boolean to a YAML 1.1 reader, the class M's Quote doc names (M:containerart/compose/compose.go:34-36); A accepts the word and writes it bare today (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:183-185). B writes the constant `always`, bare (B:internal/render/render.go:1076). M holds no default: each tool keeps its own (unless-stopped, always).
- **compose.Service.Entrypoint** `[]string` (in A, optional): `entrypoint: ["..", ".."]`, a one-line flow sequence with every token through Quote (compose.FlowList, M:containerart/compose/compose.go:46-55), after restart; omitted when nil or empty. A only, when the service carries created stores (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:158-159). Tokens: valid UTF-8, no control or invisible character.
- **compose.Service.Command** `[]string` (in A, optional): `command: [..]`, same form and checks as Entrypoint, written directly after it; omitted when nil or empty. A only: setting entrypoint drops the image CMD, so A hands both over (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:155-160).
- **compose.Service.(posture block)** `none: constant compose.Posture` (in both, required): No field. M always writes compose.Posture unchanged (M:containerart/compose/compose.go:66-70): `privileged: false`, `cap_drop:` / `- ALL`, `security_opt:` / `- no-new-privileges=true`, bare, after command. Both tools write these five lines through M today (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:194; B:internal/render/render.go:1090). Nothing in the model can turn a line off or add cap_add, devices or sysctls. artifacttest.CheckComposePosture and CheckAbsent(Widening()) keep pinning the output (M:containerart/artifacttest/artifacttest.go:33-46,100-115).
- **compose.Service.WritableRoot** `bool` (in neither, optional): M always writes `read_only: true` or `read_only: false`, a bare boolean, directly after the posture block. The zero value writes `read_only: true`, so a tool that forgets the field gets the hardened root. Neither tool writes the line today. gdu 6 gives A a root-filesystem key that defaults to read-only and is written into every artifact (M:docs/todo.md:357-358); B needs a writable root and refuses a read-only one (M:docs/todo.md:352-353), so B sets WritableRoot. artifacttest does not check the root filesystem (M:docs/todo.md:379-380).
- **compose.Service.MemLimit** `string` (in B, optional): `mem_limit: "<v>"` through Quote, after read_only; omitted when empty. Grammar the engines': a whole number with an optional b, k, m or g. B only, bare today, the tier value or 2g on a monitor (B:internal/render/render.go:1108-1117). Service-level key, not deploy.resources, for the reason B records (B:internal/render/render.go:1091-1096). Named because the design note lists resources among what a tool supplies (M:docs/design/2026-11-15-shared-deploy-api.md:254-256).
- **compose.Service.Extra** `[]compose.Setting` (in B, optional): The slot of design note 8.2 (M:docs/design/2026-11-15-shared-deploy-api.md:273-274). Written after mem_limit, in the given order, each as `<Key>:` with its value tree below it at service indent. B routes cpuset, shm_size and ulimits here (B:internal/render/render.go:1112-1126); a third tool routes stop_grace_period, init, working_dir, extra_hosts and the like. Refused: a key M writes itself, a key on the widening list (cap_add, devices, sysctls, pid, ipc, userns_mode, security_opt, privileged, network_mode, networks, volumes_from, env_file, extends and their kin), a duplicate, and a key outside ^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$.
- **compose.Service.Network** `string (docker's word: empty, bridge, host or none; the list lives in M)` (in B, optional): gdu 6 (M:docs/todo.md:371-378). Empty: the service gets `networks:` / ` - default` and the file gets the top-level default network with `driver: bridge`. bridge, host or none: `network_mode: <word>`, bare (M's own constant), and no networks key. A form that joins another namespace or a named network (container:, service:, ns:, a network name) is refused. Exactly one of the two spellings is written, so the mode is always stated. Today A writes no network line and B writes `network_mode: host` only when asked (B:internal/render/render.go:1141-1142). The host spelling is the one artifacttest.HostNetwork names (M:containerart/artifacttest/artifacttest.go:53-56). artifacttest gains the stated-mode check (M:docs/todo.md:384-386).
- **compose.Service.Ports** `[]string` (in both, optional): `ports:` then one `- "<entry>"` per entry through Quote, in the given order, after the network line. Grammar B's, the wider: [ip:]host:container[/tcp|/udp], either side a port or a lo-hi range, IPv6 in brackets (B:internal/config/artifactvalues.go:125-181); A's host:container pairs (A:internal/spec/targets.go:183-187) are a subset. Omitted when empty. Not written when Network is host or none, as B does today (B:internal/render/render.go:1141-1148). One grammar function serves the quadlet PublishPort= line too.
- **compose.Service.Environment** `[]compose.EnvVar` (in both, optional): `environment:` in mapping form, one `"<Name>": "<Value>"` per entry in the given order; omitted when empty. Both tools write the mapping form with every value through Quote, numbers and yes/no included (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:204-222; B:internal/render/render.go:1154-1160). M never sorts and does not special-case TZ: both tools put TZ first when the timezone is set, and that stays the tool's order. A duplicate name is refused. No secret value belongs here; M cannot tell, so that stays the tool's rule.
- **compose.Service.Secrets** `[]compose.Secret` (in both, optional): `secrets:` in the long syntax, `- source: "<Name>"` and ` target: "<Target>"`, in the given order, after environment; omitted when empty. Replaces compose.ServiceSecrets (M:containerart/compose/compose.go:89-103). One change: the target is quoted (CR-0004, M:docs/review/2026-11-13-decisions.md:71); it is bare in both tools today. artifacttest.CheckSecretTargets already strips the quotes (M:containerart/artifacttest/artifacttest.go:321). No mode:, uid: or gid: line: both tools take the engine default. The order is the tool's: A credentials then created stores (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:135-144), B admin, users, psk, passphrase, certificate (B:internal/render/render.go:713-752).
- **compose.Service.Configs** `[]compose.Config` (in A, optional): Service-level `configs:` with `- source: "<Name>"` and ` target: "<Target>"`, in the given order, after secrets; omitted when empty. A only: app, status, then logback and stores-init when they apply (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:236-247). The target is bare today; M quotes it like a secret target. Inline content needs Compose 2.23.1, the floor the environment secrets already set (M:containerart/compose/compose.go:72-74). A generic platform piece, so it is modelled although only A uses it.
- **compose.Service.Volumes** `[]compose.Mount` (in both, optional): `volumes:` then `- "<Source>:<Target>[:<options>]"`, the short syntax both tools write, the whole string through Quote, in the given order, after configs; omitted when empty. A: read-only binds for existing stores then libs, block omitted when there are none (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:261-269). B: one data bind, always (B:internal/render/render.go:1149-1150). Bind mounts only: a named volume is not offered.
- **compose.Service.Tmpfs** `[]compose.Tmpfs` (in A, optional): `tmpfs:` then `- "<Target>:mode=<Mode>[,uid=<U>,gid=<G>]"` through Quote, after volumes; omitted when empty. A only, one entry when it carries created stores, written bare there (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:252-257).
- **compose.Service.Healthcheck** `*compose.Healthcheck` (in both, optional): nil: no healthcheck block, the image's own check applies (B's default, B:internal/render/render.go:1127). Set: five lines in the order both tools use, test, interval, timeout, retries, start_period (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:287-294; B:internal/render/render.go:1128-1136), after tmpfs. A writes it always, B only when enabled.
- **compose.Service.Labels** `[]compose.Label` (in A, optional): The tool's own labels, written after M's five ownership labels and before the deploy-digest label, in the given order. `labels:` is the last block of the service and is always present, because M's labels are. A: solace-connector/le-mode, /role and, when a store is selected, /stores-sha256 (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:162-179). B: none of its own.
- **compose.Service.DeployDigest** `compose.DigestLabel` (in both, required): The label the redeploy rule rests on (M:containercli/containercli.go:115-128). M writes it last in labels as `"<Key>": "${<EnvVar>:-}"`: double-quoted, the '$' not doubled, so compose fills it from the child's environment. Both tools always write it (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:180-182; B:internal/render/render.go:1174-1175). A named field, not a Labels entry, so M fixes its position and can refuse a secret whose EnvVar collides with it.
- **compose.EnvVar.Name** `string` (in both, required): Written through strconv.Quote as the mapping key. Grammar ^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_.-]*$: A's are upper snake (TZ, JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS, LOGGING_SYSLOG_HOST), B's embed node names and usernames with '.' and '-' (B:internal/render/render.go:802-807,854; charset at B:internal/config/validate.go:384). The grammar has no '$', so nothing is doubled inside a key. Bare in both tools today.
- **compose.EnvVar.Value** `string` (in both, optional): Through Quote: '$' doubled so the container receives the value as written (M:containerart/compose/compose.go:19-28). Empty is allowed and written as "". Refused: invalid UTF-8, which Quote cannot carry (compose.go:38-43), and control or invisible characters. Numbers stay quoted strings, as both tools write them.
- **compose.Secret.Name** `string` (in both, required): The compose secret's name: the service's source: and the top-level key, both through Quote (M:containerart/compose/compose.go:75-77). Grammar ^[A-Za-z0-9_][A-Za-z0-9._-]*$: A's names are variable names and may lead with '_' (A's census: _GEN_TLS_<STORE>_STORE), B's are <container>-admin-password and so on (B:internal/render/render.go:522-526,719). Unique within the file.
- **compose.Secret.Target** `string` (in both, required): Absolute container path, written quoted. Refused: empty, relative (a bare name lands under the engine's own /run/secrets, compose.go:79-82), control characters. Unique across secrets, configs, volumes and tmpfs. A: /app/external/var/secrets/<name> and /app/external/var/stores/<store> (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:138,141); B: /mnt/secrets/<setting> and /mnt/certs/server/tls.pem (B:internal/render/render.go:643-664).
- **compose.Secret.EnvVar** `string` (in both, required): The variable compose's environment provider reads; written `environment: "<EnvVar>"`. Bare today (M:containerart/compose/compose.go:84-86,115); quoted in this proposal because a variable named NO, ON or Y is a boolean to a YAML 1.1 reader. Grammar ^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$. Must differ from DeployDigest.EnvVar and from every Label.Var. A passes the secret's own name (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:138), B the upper-cased folded name (B:internal/render/render.go:666-686); both stay tool choices. The value never enters the model.
- **compose.Config.Name** `string` (in A, required): The compose config's name: the service's source: and the top-level key, both through Quote. Same grammar as Secret.Name; unique within the file. A: <docker.name>-app, -status, -logback, -stores-init (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:109-116).
- **compose.Config.Target** `string` (in A, required): Absolute container path, written quoted, same rules as Secret.Target. A writes it bare (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:238-247).
- **compose.Config.Content** `string` (in A, required): Tool content: application.yml, a script, an XML file. Written as a literal block scalar under `content: |`, each non-empty line at indent 6 with '$' doubled (compose.Escape), an empty line as a bare newline with no indent (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:299-316). M adds what A's writer leaves out: a chomping indicator when the content does not end in exactly one newline (A silently adds one, A:internal/yamlwriter/yamlwriter.go:33-41), an indentation indicator when the first line starts with a space, and a refusal for what a block scalar cannot carry (a carriage return, any other control character, invalid UTF-8). For the common case, content ending in one newline, the bytes are A's of today.
- **compose.Mount.Source** `string` (in both, required): Absolute host path. Refused: relative (compose reads a bare word as a named volume), a ':' (it adds a field to the short syntax; gdu 4 refuses it in a quadlet Volume= source too, M:docs/todo.md:295-297), control characters, invalid UTF-8. Because every source is absolute, --project-directory decides nothing in the file. A resolves to absolute before rendering (A:internal/gen/gen.go:403-405); B requires an absolute dataDir.
- **compose.Mount.Target** `string` (in both, required): Absolute container path, no ':'. Unique across secrets, configs, volumes and tmpfs.
- **compose.Mount.ReadOnly** `bool` (in A, optional): true adds the `ro` option. A sets it on every bind (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:264,267); B never does.
- **compose.Mount.Relabel** `string (empty, z or Z)` (in B, optional): SELinux relabel option. B writes Z on its data directory (B:internal/render/render.go:1150); A writes none. Options are joined by a comma in fixed order: ro, then the relabel letter, giving for example "/src:/dst:ro,Z". The same Mount serves the quadlet Volume= line.
- **compose.Tmpfs.Target** `string` (in A, required): Absolute container path, no ':'. A: /app/external/classpath/truststores (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:257).
- **compose.Tmpfs.Mode** `fs.FileMode (permission bits only)` (in A, required): Written as four octal digits, `mode=0700`. Bits outside the permission bits are refused. A: 0700 (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:252).
- **compose.Tmpfs.Owner** `string (numeric uid:gid, or empty)` (in A, optional): Set: adds `,uid=<U>,gid=<G>`. Empty: neither option. A passes its run-as ids unless run-as is image (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:253-255). Digits only, both halves required.
- **compose.Healthcheck.Command** `[]string` (in both, required): The probe argv, exec form. M prepends CMD itself and writes `test: ["CMD", ..]` as a flow sequence with every token through Quote (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:289; B:internal/render/render.go:1129-1132). At least one token, none empty, valid UTF-8, no control or invisible character. A '$' is doubled, so a probe that names a shell still receives it. CMD-SHELL is not offered: neither tool uses it.
- **compose.Healthcheck.Interval** `time.Duration` (in both, required): `interval: "<n>s"`, whole seconds, quoted. Greater than zero and a whole number of seconds, else refused. A: constant 30 written 30s (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:290,297); B: a Go duration string from config, default 5s, written as given (B:internal/render/render.go:1133). Both bare today.
- **compose.Healthcheck.Timeout** `time.Duration` (in both, required): `timeout: "<n>s"`, same rule as Interval. A 10s, B default 5s.
- **compose.Healthcheck.Retries** `int` (in both, required): `retries: <n>`, bare integer, at least 1. A 3 (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:292); B from config, default 3 (B:internal/render/render.go:1135).
- **compose.Healthcheck.StartPeriod** `time.Duration` (in both, optional): `start_period: "<n>s"`, always written, zero allowed and written as "0s" (B admits zero, B:internal/config/artifactvalues.go:183-200). A 60s, B default 60s. Written in seconds so 60s does not become Go's 1m0s.
- **compose.Label.Key** `string` (in A, required): Written through strconv.Quote. Grammar: lowercase letters, digits, '.', '-', '_' and '/', alphanumeric at both ends; no '$'. Refused: one of M's five ownership keys, the DeployDigest key, a duplicate. Bare in both tools today (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:170-182).
- **compose.Label.Value** `string` (in A, optional): A literal value, through Quote. A's le-mode and role values are bare today (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:170,172). Must be empty when Var is set.
- **compose.Label.Var** `string` (in A, optional): Set: the value is `"${<Var>:-}"`, double-quoted with the '$' NOT doubled, so compose fills it from its own environment; the `:-` default keeps a hand-run compose and `down` from needing the variable (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:174-179; B:internal/render/render.go:1166-1175). Grammar ^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$. A: stores-sha256 from _GEN_TLS_STORES_SHA256 (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:55,178). Var and DeployDigest are the only places in the model that can write an interpolation; every other string is escaped.
- **compose.DigestLabel.Key** `string` (in both, required): One key per tool, passed by the caller (M:docs/review/2026-11-13-decisions.md:205): solace-connector/deploy-sha256 (A:internal/spec/defaults.go:286) and solace-util.deploy-sha256 (B:internal/render/render.go:701). Same grammar as Label.Key.
- **compose.DigestLabel.EnvVar** `string` (in both, required): The variable the tool sets to containercli.ComposeDigest of the file and the child's environment: _GEN_DEPLOY_SHA256 (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:62) and SOLACE_DEPLOY_SHA256 (B:internal/render/render.go:706). Grammar ^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$. The digest is still computed with this variable absent (M:containercli/containercli.go:127-128), and the tool still compares and asks before `up`.
- **compose.Setting.Key** `string` (in B, required): A compose service key the model has no field for, written bare at service indent after the grammar and deny checks named under Service.Extra. B: cpuset, shm_size, ulimits.
- **compose.Setting.Value** `the writer's Node (sealed: Str, Int, Bool, Seq, Map of ordered pairs)` (in B, required): A value tree, never text. Str is always written through Quote, so a '$' is doubled and nothing in the slot can interpolate; Int and Bool are bare; Seq and Map are written in block form, two more spaces per level, map keys held to ^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$. There is no raw scalar. B's three: cpuset Str("0-1") gives today's bytes (B:internal/render/render.go:1113); shm_size Str("2g") gains quotes (bare at render.go:1120); ulimits Map(nofile Map(soft Int, hard Int), memlock Int(-1), core Int(n)) gives today's bytes (render.go:1121-1126; B holds core to a decimal that fits int64, B:internal/config/artifactvalues.go:203-221).
- **labels.Owner.Tool** `string` (in neither, required): Value of app.kubernetes.io/managed-by and services.solace.com/product: solmq-conn-util, solace-util, or a third tool's name. Through Quote. Held to the Kubernetes label-value grammar on every sink, so one tool name works everywhere.
- **labels.Owner.Instance** `string` (in neither, required): Value of deploy.solace.com/instance. Checked per sink and never truncated or hashed (M:docs/todo.md:108-110): on compose the engine name grammar is enough, so B's names of up to 100 characters pass. On compose both tools pass the container name (A docker.name, B docker.container.name).
- **labels.Owner.Solution** `string` (in neither, optional): Value of services.solace.com/solution. Both tools pass accelerator (M:docs/todo.md:64-66). Proposed as a field, with the label left out when empty, because the value suits Solace Professional Services tools only (M:docs/design/2026-11-15-shared-deploy-api.md:326-327). Owner question 12.
- **labels.Owner.DevelopedBy** `string` (in neither, optional): Value of services.solace.com/developed-by. Both tools pass apac-psg. Same treatment as Solution. The version field the kube Meta needs for its annotation is not read by the compose renderer.

#### compose: extras

- B: services.<name>.ulimits (nofile.soft, nofile.hard, memlock, core). Through Service.Extra as a Map tree. The design note names it as the example for the slot (M:docs/design/2026-11-15-shared-deploy-api.md:273-274). The values are broker constants bar core (B:internal/config/config.go:1148-1153), the nested shape is compose's own, and quadlet spells the same limits differently (Ulimit= lines plus Limit*= in [Service], B:internal/render/render.go:1001-1009), so a shared typed field would serve one sink only.
- B: services.<name>.shm_size. Through Service.Extra as Str. A broker requirement fixed at 2g (B:internal/config/config.go:1149), one scalar no other workload has asked for. The value gains quotes; compose accepts a quoted size.
- B: services.<name>.cpuset. Through Service.Extra as Str. Engine-specific pinning: a service-level key with no deploy.resources equivalent (B:internal/render/render.go:1091-1096), written quoted as today because compose types it as a string (render.go:1098-1101). The monitor's swap of cpuset and mem_limit stays B's logic (render.go:1108-1111).
- A: services.<name>.labels solace-connector/le-mode. Through Service.Labels, the generic label list, as a literal value. Leader-election mode is the connector's own notion (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:162-170); M gives it no field. The value is quoted where it is bare today.
- A: services.<name>.labels solace-connector/role. Through Service.Labels. Written only for standalone and active_active, a rule only A knows (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:171-173), so A adds or leaves out the entry.
- A: services.<name>.labels solace-connector/stores-sha256. Through Service.Labels with Var set to _GEN_TLS_STORES_SHA256. Only A carries TLS stores; the generic interpolated label covers it without a second named digest (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:174-179). The deploy digest is the one named field because both tools have it.
- A: services.<name>.environment JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS, JDK_JAVA_OPTIONS, LOGGING_SYSLOG_APPNAME, LOGGING_SYSLOG_HOST, LOGGING_SYSLOG_PORT. Through Service.Environment entries in A's order. JVM and syslog settings are the connector's; M writes the framing and quoting only (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:204-222).
- B: services.<name>.environment broker settings (routername, nodetype, the redundancy_* and configsync_* pairs, system_scaling_*, username_*_globalaccesslevel, the *filepath pointers). Through Service.Environment entries in EnvPairs order (B:internal/render/render.go:779-864). The order carries meaning for B's goldens and the pointer keys must match the secret targets, which only B can pair; M keeps the given order and checks the key grammar.
- A: configs content: application.yml, status script, logback XML, stores-init script. Through Service.Configs entries; the Config type is named because inline config is a generic compose piece, but each payload stays tool content (M:docs/design/2026-11-15-shared-deploy-api.md:280-281). M owns the block-scalar framing and the '$' doubling so no payload can be left unescaped (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:299-305).
- third tool: stop_grace_period, stop_signal, init, working_dir, extra_hosts, dns, logging, pids_limit and similar posture-neutral service keys. Through Service.Extra with the value tree. None is written by A or B, so none earns a field; the slot lets a new tool ship without a module release while the deny list and artifacttest keep the posture.

#### compose: differences

- **Top-level keys and their order**
  - A: name (omitted when empty), services, configs (always), secrets (omitted when none) (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:102-124).
  - B: name (always), services, secrets (always in practice); no configs (B:internal/render/render.go:1063-1070,1185-1186).
  - Proposal: name, services, networks (only for the default network), configs, secrets. name and services always; each of the other three omitted when it has nothing.
- **Project name**
  - A: Config key docker.project-name with default solace-ibmmq-connectors, DNS-1123 label, line omitted when empty (A:internal/spec/targets.go:38-44; A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:100-104).
  - B: Derived: the container name folded to compose's grammar by ComposeProject; an empty name writes name: "" (B:internal/render/render.go:1017-1041,1063).
  - Proposal: File.Project is required and held to ^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9_-]*$; M refuses, never folds, never omits. Both tools pass their env-file entry (gdu 19; B's default becomes solace-event-brokers, M:docs/todo.md:1065-1069) and the same value goes to -p. B's ComposeProject is retired.
- **Order of the service's keys**
  - A: image, container_name, [entrypoint, command], labels, [restart], [user], posture, [ports], [environment], [secrets], configs, [tmpfs], healthcheck, [volumes] (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:150-270).
  - B: image, container_name, hostname, user, restart, posture, [cpuset], [mem_limit], shm_size, ulimits, [healthcheck], network_mode or [ports], volumes, environment, labels, secrets (B:internal/render/render.go:1071-1186).
  - Proposal: One order: image, container_name, hostname, user, restart, entrypoint, command, posture block, read_only, mem_limit, extra settings, network_mode or networks, ports, environment, secrets, configs, volumes, tmpfs, healthcheck, labels. Identity first, then process, posture, resources, network, what is mounted, the probe, and labels last so the digest label closes the service.
- **Which scalars are bare**
  - A: Bare: image, label keys and values, restart, secret and config targets, the tmpfs item, healthcheck durations, the top-level secret's variable. Quoted: names, user, ports, environment values, volume strings.
  - B: Bare: image (through Escape), restart, mem_limit, shm_size, ulimit values, healthcheck durations, network_mode, secret targets, the secret's variable, environment keys, the label key. Quoted: names, hostname, user, cpuset, ports, volume, environment values, the digest label value.
  - Proposal: One rule. Bare: the keywords and constants M spells itself (the posture block, network words, default, driver: bridge), booleans and integers. Through Quote: every string that arrives through the model, including the image, the restart word, durations, sizes, targets and the secret's variable name. Interpolations are written by M from a variable name and are the only unescaped '$'.
- **image line**
  - A: Bare and unescaped; the charset is held upstream (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:153).
  - B: Bare, through compose.Escape (B:internal/render/render.go:1072).
  - Proposal: `image: "<ref>"` through Quote, with one reference grammar in M for all three sinks that admits a tag and a digest.
- **Image reference grammar**
  - A: Per A's census, Ref() can yield name@sha256:... for a digest tag.
  - B: A digest cannot be expressed: tag grammar only (B:internal/config/artifactvalues.go:40-42).
  - Proposal: M's grammar accepts both forms; B keeps its narrower rule at its own boundary if it wants no digests.
- **user line**
  - A: Digits-only uid:gid, default 1000:0; line left out for run-as: image (A:internal/spec/runas.go:9-63; A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:191-193).
  - B: uid[:gid] with names allowed, default 1000001:0; written with no guard, so an empty value writes user: "" (B:internal/render/render.go:1075; B:internal/config/validate.go:415).
  - Proposal: User through Quote in the wider grammar; required unless UserFromImage is set. An empty user is refused, never written. Defaults stay in each tool.
- **restart line**
  - A: Config value, default unless-stopped, bare, omitted when empty; the word no is accepted (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:183-185; A:internal/validate/validate.go:1672).
  - B: Constant always, bare, always written (B:internal/render/render.go:1076).
  - Proposal: Required, checked against compose's four words, written quoted: `restart: "no"` cannot be read as a boolean. M holds no default.
- **labels block: place, keys, values**
  - A: Directly after container_name or command; keys spelled <prefix>/<name>, bare; literal values bare; interpolations bare, as in `solace-connector/deploy-sha256: ${_GEN_DEPLOY_SHA256:-}` (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen_test.go:67-70).
  - B: Second to last, before secrets; one key spelled <prefix>.<name>, bare; the interpolation hand-quoted, as in `solace-util.deploy-sha256: "${SOLACE_DEPLOY_SHA256:-}"` (B:internal/render/testdata/docker_compose_primary.golden:59-60).
  - Proposal: Last block of the service. Order: M's five ownership labels, the tool's labels as given, the deploy digest. Keys and values quoted; an interpolation in B's form, double-quoted with the '$' left single.
- **Deploy-digest key and variable names**
  - A: Key solace-connector/deploy-sha256, variable _GEN_DEPLOY_SHA256, kept clear of operator names by the _GEN_ prefix (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:57-62).
  - B: Key solace-util.deploy-sha256, variable SOLACE_DEPLOY_SHA256, kept clear of secret variables by its suffix (B:internal/render/render.go:694-706).
  - Proposal: Both stay per tool, passed in DigestLabel (decided, plan review P13). M checks the variable against every secret's EnvVar and every Label.Var instead of trusting a naming habit.
- **environment block**
  - A: Omitted when it would be empty; upper-snake keys, bare; values quoted (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:201-223).
  - B: Always written; lower-case keys that embed node names and usernames with '.' and '-', bare; values quoted (B:internal/render/render.go:1154-1160).
  - Proposal: Mapping form in the given order, omitted when empty, keys held to one grammar and quoted, values through Quote.
- **healthcheck**
  - A: Always written; constants in whole seconds spelled <n>s; sits after configs and tmpfs (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:272-297).
  - B: Written only when enabled; Go duration strings from config written as given, so 1m30s is possible; sits before the network line (B:internal/render/render.go:1127-1137).
  - Proposal: Optional pointer; time.Duration fields written as quoted whole seconds; retries a bare integer; test with CMD prepended by M; one fixed position.
- **ports**
  - A: host:container integer pairs only, quoted (A:internal/spec/targets.go:145-187; A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:195-200).
  - B: [ip:]host:container[/proto] with ranges and bracketed IPv6, quoted; none written in host mode (B:internal/config/artifactvalues.go:125-181; B:internal/render/render.go:1141-1148).
  - Proposal: []string in B's grammar, checked by M, quoted, omitted when empty and when the network is host or none.
- **Network line**
  - A: None: the service joins the project network by omission.
  - B: `network_mode: host` when asked, nothing for bridge (B:internal/render/render.go:1138-1142).
  - Proposal: Always stated (gdu 6). Empty word: the service lists the default network and the file states its bridge driver. bridge, host, none: network_mode with that word.
- **volumes**
  - A: Zero or more binds, each <src>:<dst>:ro; the block is left out when there are none; no SELinux option (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:260-269).
  - B: Exactly one bind, <dataDir>:/var/lib/solace:Z, always; never read-only (B:internal/render/render.go:1149-1150).
  - Proposal: Mount{Source, Target, ReadOnly, Relabel} in the quoted short syntax, options in the order ro then z or Z, block omitted when empty, absolute sources only, a ':' in a path refused.
- **secrets: place and naming**
  - A: Service block after environment; top-level map after configs; name and variable are the same string, the operator's variable name or a _GEN_ name (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:129-144,229).
  - B: Service block last; top-level map last; name is <container><suffix>, variable is its upper-cased fold (B:internal/render/render.go:516-526,666-686,1181-1186).
  - Proposal: One list feeds both blocks in the given order; fixed positions; target and variable quoted (CR-0004). Names and variables stay each tool's choice; M checks grammar, uniqueness and collisions.
- **Empty values**
  - A: An empty optional block is left out whole (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:146-149).
  - B: Mostly never empty by construction, but name: "" and user: "" are written for a Config built in code (B:internal/render/render.go:1027-1029,1075).
  - Proposal: A's rule for optional blocks; a required field that is empty is an error from Render. M never writes a key with an empty or null value.
- **Name grammar for the service and container**
  - A: DNS-1123 label through kube.ValidLabel (A:internal/validate/validate.go:1732).
  - B: The engines' container-name grammar with upper case, '.' and '_' (B:internal/config/validate.go:404).
  - Proposal: M checks containercli.ValidName, the grammar the engine itself applies; A keeps its narrower rule at its boundary.
- **Renderer signature and refusals**
  - A: Render(Input) string; no error, no checks, nil Docker dereferenced; refusals upstream in validate (A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:97-103).
  - B: Compose(*Config, NodeIdentity) []byte; no error; refusals upstream in config.Validate (B:internal/render/render.go:1043).
  - Proposal: File.Render() ([]byte, error). M checks every value it writes and names field, value and rule; the tools' load-time checks stay as the earlier, friendlier gate.
- **Line writer**
  - A: internal/yamlwriter.Writer with Line(indent, s) and Raw (A:internal/yamlwriter/yamlwriter.go:12-30).
  - B: fmt.Fprintf into a strings.Builder with indents typed into each format string.
  - Proposal: M's one writer (design note 8.2 layer 1), standard library only; both tools drop theirs for this artifact.
- **Writing generate output to a file**
  - A: os.WriteFile(out, content, 0600): the mode applies only when the file is created, a symlink is followed, an existing file is overwritten without a question (A:cmd/solmq-conn-util/main.go:1771-1780).
  - B: Symlink refused, overwrite confirmed, then a temp file in the same directory, chmod 0600 before the first byte, rename (B:internal/cli/helpers.go:143-199).
  - Proposal: Both write through gdu 7's atomic write at 0600 (M:docs/todo.md:400-424). The overwrite question stays in each tool. Deploy and remove keep feeding the bytes on stdin and write nothing.
- **Defaults**
  - A: restart unless-stopped, user 1000:0, project solace-ibmmq-connectors, healthcheck 30s/10s/3/60s as constants.
  - B: restart always (constant), user 1000001:0, project solace-event-brokers (decided), healthcheck 5s/5s/3/60s from config and off by default.
  - Proposal: M holds no default for any workload value: the two tools need different ones. M's own defaults are only the stated ones: the default network and read_only: true.

#### compose: owner questions

1. 1. Does a compose File hold exactly one service? Recommended: yes, File.Service and not a slice. Both tools render one, and ComposeDigest, the redeploy question, CheckComposePosture and CheckSecretTargets all assume one. A third tool with two containers renders two files into one project. A slice would bring depends_on and per-service secret subsets, which nothing has decided; v0.x can widen it later.
2. 2. Does M quote every string that arrives through the model, ending today's bare exceptions (image, restart, durations, sizes, secret and config targets, the secret's variable name)? Recommended: yes. Bare stays only for what M spells itself (the posture block, network words, default, driver: bridge), booleans and integers. It closes two live hazards: A accepts `restart: no` and writes it bare (A:internal/validate/validate.go:1672), and a secret variable named NO or ON is written bare by compose.TopSecrets (M:containerart/compose/compose.go:115). It also means M need not trust a third tool's values. Every golden changes once anyway.
3. 3. Are the keys of data maps (environment names, label keys) quoted like the service, config and secret keys already are, or left bare under a grammar? Recommended: quoted, plus a grammar that refuses '$', whitespace and control characters. One visible rule: a key that is data is quoted, a key that is a compose keyword is bare. The cost is looks only: `"TZ": "Asia/Singapore"`.
4. 4. Is the single key order for the service accepted: image, container_name, hostname, user, restart, entrypoint, command, posture, read_only, mem_limit, extra settings, network, ports, environment, secrets, configs, volumes, tmpfs, healthcheck, labels? Recommended: yes. Neither tool's order survives whole, both goldens change in the same tag for the labels anyway, and the digest label ends the service block.
5. 5. How is the default network written, and is it labelled? Recommended: the service states `networks:` / `- default` and the file states `networks:` / `default:` / `driver: bridge`; the service line gives artifacttest one place to read the mode, the top-level block states the driver as decided (M:docs/todo.md:377-378). No labels on the network. This needs your ruling because gdu 1 says neither tool renders a top-level network (M:docs/todo.md:104-105) and gdu 6 now adds one: with a shared project (gdu 19) two instances define the same network, and an instance label on it would differ between them.
6. 6. When the network word is host or none and the tool still passes ports, does M leave the ports out or refuse? Recommended: leave them out and say so in the field's doc. B keeps the list in the env file in host mode on purpose and writes none (B:internal/render/render.go:1141-1148; B:internal/config/artifactvalues.go:223-232); a refusal would make B strip the list before every render.
7. 7. With -p on every compose call, does the file still carry `name:`? Recommended: yes, always, required, in compose's own grammar, with no fold. `generate` output is run by hand without -p, and a reviewer sees the project. The tool passes the one File.Project value to both the renderer and the client. B's ComposeProject fold and its empty `name: ""` go.
8. 8. Must a tool state the user explicitly, and does M refuse root? Recommended: User is required unless UserFromImage is set, so a missing user is an error and never `user: ""` or a silent root. M does not refuse uid 0 itself: UserFromImage already lets an image's root through, so whether root is acceptable stays each tool's rule. The grammar is B's wider one (names allowed); A keeps digits only at its boundary.
9. 9. Does M always state the root filesystem? Recommended: yes, `read_only: true` or `read_only: false` on every service, with the model field named WritableRoot so the zero value is the read-only one. A gets its decided default with no extra code, B sets WritableRoot and its file shows `read_only: false` to a reviewer. This adds a line to B's file that gdu 6 did not ask for.
10. 10. What may go through the Extra slot? Recommended: a typed value tree only (quoted strings, integers, booleans, lists, maps; no raw text), and a deny list rather than an allow list: every key M writes itself plus the widening keys. An allow list would need a module release for each new key and defeat the slot; the tree cannot carry an interpolation or a second key, and artifacttest stays the backstop on what a tool ships.
11. 11. Which settings only B writes get a named field? Recommended: hostname and mem_limit are named (generic, and the design note lists names and resources); cpuset, shm_size and ulimits go through Extra. The SELinux relabel letter is a named Mount field because quadlet's Volume= takes the same option.
12. 12. Two label points. (a) Does a compose service carry no version or lib label, although design note 8.2 speaks of labels and annotations together? Recommended: confirm none, as gdu 1 decided (M:docs/todo.md:67-70); otherwise every upgrade recreates every container. (b) Are services.solace.com/solution and developed-by fields of the Owner, left out when empty? Recommended: yes; both tools pass accelerator and apac-psg, and a tool outside Professional Services passes nothing (M:docs/design/2026-11-15-shared-deploy-api.md:326-327).
13. 13. Is the restart policy required, with no default in M? Recommended: yes. Both tools state one today and their defaults differ (unless-stopped, always). A third tool must choose; an omitted line would inherit docker's `no` silently.
14. 14. Are healthcheck timings limited to whole seconds? Recommended: yes: time.Duration in the model, written as `"<n>s"`, a fraction of a second refused. It gives one spelling for A's constants and B's config strings, keeps 60s from becoming 1m0s, and suits quadlet's HealthInterval= too.
15. 15. What does M do with inline config content a literal block cannot carry (a carriage return, another control character, invalid UTF-8), and with content that does not end in exactly one newline? Recommended: refuse the first with an error naming the config and the offset; for the second write the chomping indicator so the container gets the bytes the tool passed. A today silently adds a final newline (A:internal/yamlwriter/yamlwriter.go:33-41).
16. 16. Does this tag offer named volumes, extra or external networks, or a mode, uid or gid on a secret or config? Recommended: no. Neither tool writes them, and a named volume or network is an engine object that needs ownership labels and a removal rule nobody has decided (gdu 1, 16, 18). A tool that needs one waits for a module change.
17. 17. Which pieces of package compose stay exported once File renders? Recommended, by the rule for builders a client covers: Quote and Escape stay (a tool's tests and its own content use them), Posture stays because artifacttest and both tools' posture tests read it, Secret stays as a model type; FlowList, ServiceSecrets and TopSecrets become unexported. To be confirmed name by name in the surface review.
18. 18. Is Render the only gate, or does M also export its grammar checks? Recommended: both. Render refuses on its own, and M exports the same checks (project name, port entry, restart word, network word, image reference, variable name) plus File.Validate() so a tool can refuse at load with its own field names, before any question is asked.

#### compose: census notes

- A: Roots: A = A, M = M. Files opened: A internal/dockergen/dockergen.go (all 316 lines), A internal/gen/gen.go (GenerateDocker 403-464 and helpers), A internal/gen/stores.go:240-320, A internal/yamlwriter/yamlwriter.go, parts of A internal/spec, statusscript, storescript, logback, tls, validate, cmd/solmq-conn-util/main.go, and M containerart/compose/compose.go plus M containercli/containercli.go:84-135. Not opened: the goldens and dockergen_test.go, so byte-level claims come from the renderer source only. SCOPE - Tool A writes exactly one compose artifact: one file, one service. There is no second compose document, no override file and no env file (credentials travel in the compose child's environment). - The four inlined payloads (application.yml, status script, logback XML, stores-init script) are tool content; I list the wrapper lines, not the payload lines. TOP-LEVEL ORDER (fixed) name (optional) -> services -> configs -> secrets (optional). No leading "---", no version: key, file ends with a newline. SERVICE KEY ORDER (fixed, dockergen.go:150-270) image, container_name, [entrypoint, command], labels, [restart], [user], privileged, cap_drop, security_opt, [ports], [environment], [secrets], configs, [tmpfs], healthcheck, [volumes]. - labels order: le-mode, role, stores-sha256, deploy-sha256. - environment order: TZ, JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS, JDK_JAVA_OPTIONS, LOGGING_SYSLOG_APPNAME, _HOST, _PORT. - configs order, both in the service and at top level: app, status, logback, stores-init. - secrets order, both places, from one function (secretList, dockergen.go:135-144): credentials in consolidate Model.Secrets order, then created stores in selected-store order. - volumes order: existing stores, then libs. - tmpfs sits between configs and healthcheck because it is written inside the stores-init branch (dockergen.go:245-258). - Empty-block rule (dockergen.go:146-149): a sub-block with nothing in it is omitted whole (name, entrypoint/command, restart, user, ports, environment, secrets, tmpfs, volumes). labels, the posture lines, configs and healthcheck are always present. INDENTATION Service key at indent 2, service fields at 4, list items and map entries at 6, a list item's second key at 8. M's Posture, ServiceSecrets and TopSecrets hard-code indent 4 (compose.go:5-7,66-70,97-100), which is why they drop into A's writer through w.Raw. QUOTING POLICY AS WRITTEN - Through compose.Quote: project name, service key, container_name, user, each port, every environment value, every config/secret name, each volume string. FlowList quotes each item of entrypoint, command and healthcheck.test. - Bare: image, label keys and values, restart, secret targets, config targets, the tmpfs item, healthcheck durations and retries, the top-level secret's environment: name, privileged. - Two label values are intentional compose interpolations ("${_GEN_TLS_STORES_SHA256:-}", "${_GEN_DEPLOY_SHA256:-}") and must never pass through Escape or Quote (compose.go:26-27 says the same). An M Service model needs a way to mark a value as raw/interpolated. - Block-scalar bodies go through compose.Escape per line; blank lines are written with no indent at all (dockergen.go:309-315). - Possible hazard, not verified against compose: restart is written bare and validate accepts the word "no" (validate.go:1672), giving "restart: no", which a YAML 1.1 reader takes as a boolean. M's own Quote doc (compose.go:34-36) names this class of problem. Worth a decision when M takes over the line. WHAT THE RENDERER REFUSES - dockergen.Render refuses nothing: it has no error return and no validation (dockergen.go:97). It also dereferences in.Docker without a nil check (dockergen.go:102,151), so the caller must guarantee it. - All refusals are upstream in gen.GenerateDocker, which returns an empty plan on any of: no docker: section (gen.go:412-414); any validate.Run error (gen.go:415-423); status password resolution failure (gen.go:425-428); model build failure such as a secret-name collision (gen.go:429-433); run-as that does not parse (gen.go:435-438, 781-787). - Validation that guards values written bare or as-is into the file: docker.name and docker.project-name must be DNS-1123 labels (validate.go:1732,1814); docker.restart must match the compose policy regex (validate.go:1685); ports 1-65535 (validate.go:1745, 1627-1634); image.repo/name/tag and timezone held to SafeToken (validate.go:1528,1552); java-options refused on $, quotes, backslash, backtick, control and invisible characters (validate.go:1472, 1486-1515); docker.libs.dir required when libs is set and held to safeHostPath (validate.go:1756-1758); each existing.pkcs12 path held to safeHostPath (validate.go:1781); store names lowercase/digits/hyphens, max 63 (validate.go:452,462); an -env name starting with _GEN_ is refused (validate.go:411-418); a store held only in a kubernetes Secret is refused for docker (validate.go:1219-1226); retired keys docker.image, docker.timezone, docker.secrets, docker.stores, docker.libs.mount-path are refused (validate.go:1714-1754). - safeHostPath checks the path as written in env.yaml, not the absolute path res.abs produces (validate.go:1161-1163); the resolved path is protected only by compose.Quote. - I did not open the check that restricts the leader-election mode word, which lands bare as a label value. COMMENTS WRITTEN INTO THE ARTIFACT - None. Render emits no YAML comment line, no header and no generated-by notice. - The inlined payloads carry their own comments as data inside block scalars: the stores-init script's "# solmq-conn-util: decode..." header (storescript.go:62-66), the status script's usage header (statusscript.go:104-121), and XML comments in the logback config (logback.go:35-41). WHAT THE FILE NEVER CONTAINS (whole renderer read) No networks or network_mode line (the service joins compose's default project bridge network implicitly), no read_only, no resource limits, no ulimits, no init, no hostname, no logging driver, no depends_on, no named volumes, no tool/lib/version labels or annotations. Design section 8.2 has M writing a network line and lib labels, so each of those is a new line in A's file and a golden change. SINK AND FILE MODE - generate --platform docker with -o: os.WriteFile(out, content, 0o600) (main.go:1776; call at main.go:496). Without -o: fmt.Print to stdout (main.go:1772-1774). - deploy and remove: the text is never written to disk (main.go:788-790). It goes on stdin to "<docker.command> compose -f - --project-directory <envDir> up -d" or "... down" through containercli.ComposeUp / ComposeDown (A internal/runner/runner.go:174-190; M containercli/containercli.go:88-113), with the secrets and stores in the child's environment only. - deploy also hashes the text plus that environment with containercli.ComposeDigest and passes the result as _GEN_DEPLOY_SHA256 (main.go:782-786), so any byte change in the rendered file recreates a running container after asking. - validate's host checks call GenerateDocker and discard the text (main.go:1248). - The 0600 mode matters: per the comment at gen.go:819-822 the status account's password is a literal in application.yml, which is inlined in this file; credential values themselves are never in it. M PIECES IN USE TODAY compose.Quote, compose.Escape, compose.FlowList, compose.Posture, compose.Secret, compose.ServiceSecrets, compose.TopSecrets. Everything else, including the line writer (A internal/yamlwriter), is the tool's own. WHAT AN M File/Service MODEL MUST COVER FOR TOOL A Top-level name; service key and container_name as separate fields that happen to be equal; entrypoint and command as flow lists; ordered labels with raw (unescaped) values; optional restart and user; ordered environment; long-syntax secrets with absolute targets; service configs as source/target pairs plus top-level configs with inline content: block scalars; a tmpfs entry with mount options; an exec-form healthcheck with interval, timeout, retries and start_period; read-only short-syntax bind volumes; and omission of every empty block.
- B: SCOPE. Tool B renders exactly one compose artifact: render.Compose (B:internal/render/render.go:1043-1188). It is the only non-test file importing containerart/compose (render.go:41). One file, one service, no `version:`, no top-level `networks:` or `volumes:`, no `env_file:`, no `deploy:` block, no `command:`/`entrypoint:`. Goldens: internal/render/testdata/docker_compose_primary.golden, docker_compose_standalone.golden, docker_compose_healthcheck.golden (primary golden read in full; it matches the field order listed). WHAT M WRITES TODAY vs WHAT THE TOOL WRITES. M pieces in use: compose.Quote, compose.Escape (image only), compose.FlowList (healthcheck test), compose.Posture (5 lines), compose.Secret + compose.ServiceSecrets + compose.TopSecrets. Everything else is tool-side fmt.Fprintf: name:, the service key, image, container_name, hostname, user, restart, cpuset, mem_limit, shm_size, the whole ulimits block, the healthcheck block layout, network_mode/ports, volumes, environment, labels. For the planned compose File/Service model (design note 8.2 layer 3) B needs slots for: top-level `name:` with a project-name fold (ComposeProject, render.go:1030); cpuset (quoted); mem_limit (bare); shm_size; ulimits with a nested nofile soft/hard map plus scalar memlock and core; healthcheck with CMD-form test; a bind volume with the `:Z` SELinux suffix; environment in MAPPING form with caller-fixed order; a label whose value is an UN-escaped `${VAR:-}` interpolation; restart policy. QUOTING INCONSISTENCIES A SHARED WRITER MUST REPRODUCE OR DELIBERATELY CHANGE (each changes the goldens): - image: Escape only, bare (render.go:1072), while every name goes through Quote. - mem_limit, shm_size, memlock, core, healthcheck interval/timeout/start_period, restart, network_mode: bare (render.go:1076, 1116, 1120, 1125-1126, 1133-1136, 1142). - secret target: bare; top-level secret `environment:` var: bare (M compose.go:100, 115). - environment KEYS: bare (render.go:1159); they embed redundancy node names and usernames, safe only because config holds those to identRE / brokerUsernameRE at load. Environment VALUES are all quoted, including numbers and yes/no. - the digest label value is the one scalar that must skip Escape (render.go:1170-1175; M compose.go:26-27 says the same). - service key and container_name use the raw name; only `name:` is folded by ComposeProject. ORDERING RULES. Top level: name, services, secrets. Service keys in fixed order: image, container_name, hostname, user, restart, [Posture: privileged, cap_drop, security_opt], cpuset?, mem_limit?, shm_size, ulimits(nofile.soft, nofile.hard, memlock, core), healthcheck?(test, interval, timeout, retries, start_period), network_mode XOR ports?, volumes, environment, labels, secrets. Network: host mode writes `network_mode: host` and no ports; bridge writes no network line at all and `ports:` only when the list is non-empty (render.go:1138-1148) -- note for gdu 6 that B states bridge by omission, not by a line. Environment order is EnvPairs order (render.go:779-864): TZ?, routername, nodetype, then HA block (activestandbyrole?, redundancy_enable yes, configsync_enable, 6 group pairs primary/backup/monitor, 2 TLS enables?) or standalone `redundancy_enable: no`, then tls_servercertificate_filepath?, 8 scaling pairs, admin access level, per-user access levels (config order), then the *filepath pointers in containerSecretSpecs order. Secret order (render.go:562-598, 713-752): admin password, additional users in config order, redundancy psk, TLS passphrase, server certificate bundle LAST (comment at 725: so no index or golden moves). The certificate is deliberately not a containerSecretSpecs entry, so its *filepath pair is emitted separately and earlier (824) than the other pointers (861). Monitor role swaps only cpuset and mem_limit (render.go:1108-1111). WHAT THE RENDERER REFUSES. Nothing: Compose is a pure function returning []byte with no error (render.go:1043); it does no I/O. An empty container name is rendered as `name: ""` on purpose (render.go:1027-1029). All refusals are upstream in config.Validate, at load, on the values written as-is: - image.registry/repo/tag held to the OCI reference grammar; a digest is not expressible (internal/config/artifactvalues.go:86-105; called from validate.go:107). - timezone: invalid UTF-8, control, invisible, whitespace, execguard.UnsafeChars (so `$`) refused (artifactvalues.go:56-81; validate.go:114). - docker.container.name: identRE charset (validate.go:384, 579-589, 1591-1600), must start alphanumeric (engineContainerNameRE, validate.go:404, 1612-1618), max 100 chars (validate.go:410, 1619-1623). redundancy.primary/backup/monitor.name: identRE. - runUser: uid[:gid] charset (validate.go:415, 1625-1628). mem: integer + b|k|m|g (validate.go:1629-1635; scaling.go:60). cpuset: [0-9,-] list or range, not inverted, count must equal the scaling tier cores (validate.go:427, 1636-1660). monitorCpuset: same grammar, exactly one cpu (validate.go:1663-1678). - dataDir must be an absolute host path (validate.go:1575-1580) and passes CheckHostPath (validate.go:310-312, 330). - network.mode must be bridge or host (validate.go:1704-1708); every network.ports entry must be [ip:]host:container[/tcp|/udp], checked even in host mode (artifactvalues.go:132-181, 226-232). - healthCheck.interval/timeout/startPeriod must be Go durations, non-negative, zero only for startPeriod (artifactvalues.go:187-200, 233-245); healthCheck.cmd tokens: no empty token, valid UTF-8, no control or invisible character, shell syntax allowed (validate.go:351-373, 1683-1690); the built-in probe is version-gated on the image tag (validate.go:1691, 1720-1733). - ulimits.core: -1, 0 or a plain decimal that fits int64 (artifactvalues.go:204-221, 247-251). Retired keys shmSize, ulimits.nofile, ulimits.memlock are refused by name (validate.go:240-242). - tls.cert and tls.certKey must be set together (validate.go:1568-1573). additionalUsers usernames: broker grammar, not admin/monitor, unique, and no two folding to one EnvVar (validate.go:1262-1285). - At deploy only (not render): render.SecretPreflight refuses an empty credential value, naming the config key (render.go:1198-1223). COMMENTS WRITTEN INTO THE ARTIFACT. None. No header, no generated-by line, no inline comment, no blank lines; the file ends with the newline of the last top-level secret entry. SECRET VALUES. No secret value is ever in the file. Each secret is an environment-provider secret: the file names the variable, and the value is passed only in the compose child's environment as EnvVar=Value (internal/container/manager.go:199-209). The digest label variable SOLACE_DEPLOY_SHA256 is set the same way to containercli.ComposeDigest(file, env), computed before the variable is appended (manager.go:886-892); the running container's label is read back via inspect to predict a recreate (manager.go:930-940). SINKS AND FILE MODE. 1. Deploy (docker): never written to disk. The bytes go to compose on stdin through M containercli.ComposeUp -> `<compose> -f - --project-directory <dir> up -d [--force-recreate]`, env for the child alone (internal/container/manager.go:886, 918; M M:containercli/containercli.go:90-108). projectDir is the env file's directory or "." (manager.go:183-188). No `-p`: the project name comes from the file's own `name:` so COMPOSE_PROJECT_NAME still overrides (render.go:1049-1062). 2. Remove (docker): re-rendered with the Primary identity and fed on stdin to containercli.ComposeDown, secrets defined as placeholders (manager.go:1126-1135; M containercli.go:111-113). 3. `generate` (opCtrGenArtifact, internal/cli/ops_container.go:406-415): emitOrWrite. With no --out, the bytes go to stdout (internal/cli/helpers.go:19-22, 144-146). With --out: a symlink target is refused, an existing file needs the Overwrite confirmation, then the text is written to a temp file `.solace-*.tmp` in the same directory, chmod 0600 before the first byte, and renamed over the target (helpers.go:147-199). So the on-disk mode is 0600. TESTS THAT PIN THE ARTIFACT (render_test.go, lines seen by grep only): artifacttest.AssertComposePosture (1239), AssertAbsent with Widening() (1262), HostNetwork checks (1276-1288), AssertSecretTargets (1537). Files read: B:internal/render/render.go (1-70, 500-889, 1000-1304); internal/config/config.go, load.go, roles.go, validate.go, artifactvalues.go (ranges cited); internal/cli/helpers.go, internal/cli/ops_container.go; internal/container/manager.go (180-215, 850-945, 1120-1145); M:containerart/compose/compose.go (whole); containercli/containercli.go (88-151); design note sections 1, 3, 4, 7, 8. Not verified: line numbers inside render_test.go beyond the grep hits; the exact compose-side behaviour of any key (nothing was run).

### compose: verifier

- **compose.Mount.Source and difference 'volumes' (absolute only, a ':' refused)**
  - Was: Absolute host path; relative and any ':' refused. Presented as covering both tools.
  - Should be: Not representable for A as A works today. A's host-path gate admits ':' and backslash on purpose for Windows-authored paths, A resolves bind sources with filepath.IsAbs/Join on the machine it runs on, and its docker deploy has a Windows branch, so a resolved source is C:\dir\file. Make it an owner question: either M admits exactly one drive-letter root (^[A-Za-z]:[\\/]) and no other colon, or A's docker platform refuses a ':' at load in the same step and Windows docker hosts are dropped. M must not test absoluteness with filepath.IsAbs (the answer would depend on the machine running the tool; B wrote IsAbsHostPath for that reason). For B the refusal is a tightening: B accepts a drive-letter dataDir as absolute and writes it into the volume line with no guard.
  - Evidence: A:internal/validate/validate.go:1159-1166; A:cmd/solmq-conn-util/main.go:758,1762-1767; A:internal/gen/stores.go:388-389; B:internal/config/hostpath.go:41-46,126-153; B:internal/render/render.go:1150
- **compose.Mount.ReadOnly (zero value is read-write)**
  - Was: ReadOnly bool; true adds 'ro'. A sets it on every bind, B never.
  - Should be: Invert it so the zero value is the hardened one (for example Writable bool; zero writes ':ro', B sets Writable on its data directory). A hard-codes ':ro' on every bind today, so it cannot be forgotten; with ReadOnly a missed field during adoption makes the truststore files and the libs directory writable from the container, and the libs directory is on the connector's classpath. The model applies exactly this reasoning to WritableRoot and not to the mounts.
  - Evidence: A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:260-269; A:internal/validate/validate.go:1746-1752; A:internal/spec/targets.go:79
- **compose.Service.WritableRoot and owner question 9 (M always writes read_only, default true)**
  - Was: M writes read_only: true or false on every service; zero value read-only; B sets WritableRoot and its file shows read_only: false.
  - Should be: Three problems. (1) It overrides a decision: gdu 6 leaves the root filesystem to each tool and keeps it out of artifacttest. (2) It breaks a pinned B rule the census did not list: B's test fails any compose artifact carrying 'read_only:' in any spelling, and B's audit grep 6 counts any hit in a golden as a finding. So read_only: false in B's file needs an owner ruling that rewrites B's rule 6, its test and its audit table, not just a golden change. (3) Unverified hazard, from memory of compose and not run here: compose delivers environment-provider secrets and inline content: configs by copying files into the created container, and the engine refuses a copy into a read-only root outside a volume. If that holds, read_only: true fails 'up' for A (credentials, application.yml, status script) and every tool using M's own secret delivery must set WritableRoot, so the hardened zero value is not reachable. Recommended: a tri-state the tool states (line written only when stated; A true per gdu 6, B none) and a real-engine row in docs/smoke.md before any default is fixed.
  - Evidence: M:docs/todo.md:352-353,379-380,393; M:docs/review/2026-11-13-decisions.md:168-169; B:internal/render/render_test.go:1319-1345; B:docs/container-security.md:40,236-244,322,336; A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:224-247
- **compose.Service.Network and difference 'Network line' (the word bridge)**
  - Was: Empty: project network. bridge, host, none: network_mode: <word>. B described only as 'network_mode: host when asked, nothing for bridge'.
  - Should be: State the mapping for B, because the same word changes meaning. B defaults docker.network.mode to 'bridge' at load, accepts only bridge or host, and renders bridge as no line, which is the project network. Passed through unchanged, every B deploy (default included) becomes network_mode: bridge: docker's shared default bridge, which leaves the project network (gdu 6 says so) and puts the broker's unpublished listeners on the bridge every unrelated container on the host uses. The model must say: B drops its 'bridge' default so the empty word reaches M, and an env file that still says bridge is either refused or documented as the new meaning. B's own comment justifies the default as the non-widening choice, so a silent move to docker0 weakens what B does today.
  - Evidence: B:internal/config/load.go:255-260; B:internal/config/validate.go:1704-1708; B:internal/render/render.go:1138-1148; M:docs/todo.md:364-367,372-378
- **compose.Service.Extra, compose.Setting and owner question 10 (deny list plus artifacttest as backstop)**
  - Was: Any key outside a deny list (keys M writes, plus cap_add, devices, sysctls, pid, ipc, userns_mode, security_opt, privileged, network_mode, networks, volumes_from, env_file, extends 'and their kin'); artifacttest stays the backstop.
  - Should be: An allow list, or at least a complete enumerated deny list with artifacttest widened in the same step. Today the key set is closed: both renderers hard-code every key, so no tool can add one. The slot opens it, and the named backstop does not cover compose spellings: Widening() has no token for userns_mode, uts, cgroup, cgroup_parent, group_add, device_cgroup_rules, runtime, gpus, volumes_from, use_api_socket, build, label_file, links, oom_kill_disable, storage_opt, or a post_start/pre_stop hook with user: root. 'And their kin' is not a rule a test can pin. A new compose key is then allowed by default, the opposite of stating the posture. Also: the Map key grammar ^[a-z][a-z0-9_]*$ cannot express logging.options keys such as max-size although logging is the model's own example, and Str in the slot needs the same UTF-8 and control-character refusal as EnvVar.Value (Quote cannot carry invalid UTF-8). Int must be int64 for B's core limit.
  - Evidence: M:containerart/artifacttest/artifacttest.go:33-46; A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:150-270; B:internal/render/render.go:1070-1186; M:containerart/compose/compose.go:38-43; B:internal/config/artifactvalues.go:203-221
- **compose.Secret.Target, compose.Config.Target, Mount.Target, Tmpfs.Target ('unique across secrets, configs, volumes and tmpfs')**
  - Was: Targets must be unique; nothing about one target lying under another.
  - Should be: Add a nesting rule: refuse a secret or config target that equals or lies under a Mount or Tmpfs target (a directory mount shadows it, and nothing reports it), and a Mount or Tmpfs target that lies under a secret or config target. Keep the one nesting A relies on: a Mount target under a Tmpfs target (existing stores inside the truststores tmpfs). Both tools hold this today by construction and say why: A moved the status script out from under the libs volume because the volume mounted over it (the healthcheck then runs whatever the host directory holds), and B pins that no secret nests with the certificate mount. Uniqueness alone does not keep either property.
  - Evidence: A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:230-235,248-251; A:internal/storescript/storescript.go:26-27; B:internal/render/render_test.go:1544-1573
- **compose.Secret.EnvVar, compose.DigestLabel.EnvVar, compose.Label.Var (and difference 'secrets: place and naming')**
  - Was: Grammar ^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$; a secret's EnvVar must differ from the digest variable and every Label.Var.
  - Should be: Two rules are missing that B enforces today. (1) No two secrets share an EnvVar: B refuses usernames that fold to one variable because one user's password would be fed to both; the model lists uniqueness for Name and Target only. (2) Reserved names: the tool sets EnvVar=value in the compose child's environment, so a variable the child or its loader reads must be refused. B pins this for the digest variable (PATH, LD_PRELOAD, LD_LIBRARY_PATH, IFS, BASH_ENV, SHELL); M should refuse at least those plus HOME, LD_*, DOCKER_* and COMPOSE_* for all three fields, since a third tool's names are not trusted and A passes operator-chosen -env names straight into this slot.
  - Evidence: B:internal/config/validate.go:1279-1292; B:internal/render/servercert_test.go:324-342; B:internal/container/manager.go:191-209; A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:135-144
- **compose.Config.Content and owner question 15 (what a literal block cannot carry)**
  - Was: Refuse a carriage return, any other control character and invalid UTF-8; add an indentation indicator when the first line starts with a space.
  - Should be: The list misses the three other line breaks the YAML reader honours: NEL U+0085, LS U+2028 and PS U+2029. Inside a content line any of them ends the line, the rest starts at column 0, the block scalar ends and payload text is read as compose keys. M's own helpers would let them through: execguard.IsControl excludes U+0085 and IsInvisible is category Cf only, while LS and PS are Zl and Zp. Refuse CR, NEL, LS, PS, C0 other than TAB, DEL, C1 and U+FEFF, and decide TAB explicitly (IsControl refuses it; shell and XML payloads may carry it). 'First line' must read 'first non-empty line'. A has the same gap today (it splits on LF only), so this is a hole the model claims to close and does not. Quoted scalars are safe: strconv.Quote escapes all three.
  - Evidence: A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:299-316; A:internal/yamlwriter/yamlwriter.go:33-41; M:execguard/execguard.go:194-204; $GOMODCACHE/gopkg.in/yaml.v3@v3.0.1/yamlprivateh.go:124-130
- **File.Render error text ('one error naming field, value and rule')**
  - Was: Every refusal names the field, the value and the rule.
  - Should be: Carve out Config.Content: an error names the config and an offset, never content bytes. A's application.yml is inlined as a config and carries the status account's password as a literal, derived from the deployment's credentials on docker; that is why A never writes the file to disk on deploy. Say the same in the Result and hook rules for any path that could print the rendered file.
  - Evidence: A:internal/runner/runner.go:153-158; A:internal/gen/gen.go:854-865
- **compose.Service.Image reference grammar (tag optional)**
  - Was: [registry[:port]/]path[:tag][@sha256:<64 hex>], tag and digest both optional.
  - Should be: Require a tag or a digest. As written an untagged reference passes Render and resolves to latest. Both tools refuse that today: A by a load check that says an untagged image pins nothing, B because image.tag is required and Ref always joins one. With the grammar in M and trusted by a third tool, the pin requirement should move with it.
  - Evidence: A:internal/spec/image.go:61-75; A:internal/validate/validate.go:1539-1543; B:internal/config/config.go:311-316; B:internal/config/artifactvalues.go:40-42,83-85
- **Value domains narrower than the tools' load gates (Healthcheck.Interval/Timeout/StartPeriod, Healthcheck.Retries, Hostname, User, MemLimit)**
  - Was: Presented as B's grammars or as supersets of them.
  - Should be: List each as a narrowing B must mirror at load in the same step, or Render becomes the first gate and fails after questions were asked. Durations: B accepts any positive Go duration (500ms, 1.5s) and writes it as given; the model refuses a fraction of a second. Retries: B has no load check at all, the model wants at least 1. Hostname: B's node names follow identRE with no first-character rule; the model wants an alphanumeric first. User: the cited runUserRE has no first-character rule either, so 'B's wider grammar with an alphanumeric or _ first character' is not B's. MemLimit: B's rule is case-insensitive with a mandatory unit (2G passes, a bare number does not); the model says an optional lower-case unit. All are tightenings, none weakens posture.
  - Evidence: B:internal/config/artifactvalues.go:183-200; B:internal/config/load.go:351; B:internal/config/config.go:1118; B:internal/config/validate.go:384,415,1625-1628; B:internal/config/scaling.go:60
- **compose.Mount.Source note: '--project-directory decides nothing in the file'**
  - Was: Because every source is absolute, the project directory decides nothing.
  - Should be: It still decides interpolation input: compose reads a .env from the project directory, and both tools pass the env file's own directory for that reason. A .env there can fill the ${VAR:-} label variables whenever the tool does not set them (remove, a hand-run of generate output) and can set compose's own variables. With every path absolute, the client could pass a neutral directory instead; that belongs with containercli.Client.ComposeUp and ComposeDown.
  - Evidence: B:internal/container/manager.go:181-188; A:internal/runner/runner.go:165-168
- **compose.File.Service note 'M never writes a key that holds null or an empty string' against compose.EnvVar.Value 'empty is written as ""'**
  - Was: Two statements that contradict each other for an empty environment value.
  - Should be: Keep the EnvVar rule and pin it with a test: an empty Value is written as a quoted empty string, never as a bare key. A key-only environment entry makes compose take the value from the environment compose itself runs with (compose behaviour, not verified here), and that is where both tools put the secret values.
  - Evidence: B:internal/container/manager.go:191-209; A:internal/runner/runner.go:170-173

Missed:

- (A) Host paths: safeHostPath admits ':' and backslash on purpose for Windows-authored paths, the bind source is resolved with filepath.IsAbs/Join on the running machine, and docker deploy has a Windows branch. The census says only 'held to safeHostPath' and 'absolute host path'. [A:internal/validate/validate.go:1159-1166; A:cmd/solmq-conn-util/main.go:758,1762-1767; A:internal/gen/stores.go:388-389]
- (A) Placement invariant behind the targets: the config targets (application.yml, status script, stores-init) are kept outside every volume mount so no mount can shadow them, and the existing-store binds are meant to land inside the truststores tmpfs. The census lists the lines, not the rule. [A:internal/dockergen/dockergen.go:230-235,248-251; A:internal/storescript/storescript.go:26-27]
- (A) The project directory is env.yaml's directory so that an .env file there keeps resolving: compose reads it, and it can fill the two ${...:-} label variables when the child environment does not set them. [A:internal/runner/runner.go:165-168]
- (A) Test pins the census did not open: Spring ${NAME} placeholders in application.yml must be written as $$ or compose substitutes the real credential into the document; env_file is never emitted; every artifact for run-as '', 1234:5678, 0 and image carries no Widening() and no HostNetwork() token (so uid 0 is an accepted run-as and host networking is forbidden in every shape today). [A:internal/dockergen/dockergen_test.go:132-134,226-227,496-527; A:internal/gen/gen_runas_test.go:127-147]
- (A) Image.Ref() returns an untagged reference when the tag is empty; only the load check refuses it. The census gives two forms and no untagged one. [A:internal/spec/image.go:70-72; A:internal/validate/validate.go:1539-1543]
- (B) A test and an audit rule forbid 'read_only:' in every compose shape (rule 6: read-only root not supported; grep 6: any hit in a golden is a finding). Missing from the census's list of tests that pin the artifact. [B:internal/render/render_test.go:1319-1345; B:docs/container-security.md:40,236-244,322,336]
- (B) TestDeployDigestEnvVarIsFixedAndDistinct: the digest variable must not be a name the child's loader reads (PATH, LD_PRELOAD, LD_LIBRARY_PATH, IFS, BASH_ENV, SHELL) and no secret's EnvVar may equal it, whatever the container is called. [B:internal/render/servercert_test.go:324-342]
- (B) TestSecretsAndCertDoNotNest: secret mount paths are unique and none nests with the certificate mount, because a directory mount shadows anything under it. [B:internal/render/render_test.go:1544-1573]
- (B) docker.network.mode is defaulted to the word 'bridge' at load (justified as the non-widening choice) and the renderer turns that word into no line. The census records the accepted words and the omission, not the default word. [B:internal/config/load.go:255-260; B:internal/config/validate.go:1704-1708; B:internal/render/render.go:1138-1148]
- (B) projectDir is also where compose reads a .env from. The census says only that it is the env file's directory or '.'. [B:internal/container/manager.go:181-188]
- (B) dataDir 'absolute' includes a drive-letter root, so the volume source can carry ':' and backslash. hostpath.go says the colon is handled where the mount line is written; the compose mount line has no such handling. [B:internal/config/hostpath.go:41-46,126-153; B:internal/render/render.go:1150]
- (B) healthCheck.retries has no load-time check: it is defaulted when zero and written with %d, so a negative value reaches the file. Not in the census's list of refusals. [B:internal/config/config.go:1118; B:internal/config/load.go:351; B:internal/render/render.go:1135]
- (B) Grammars looser than the census wording: mem is case-insensitive with a mandatory unit; runUser admits names, has no first-character rule and does not refuse uid 0; node names (hostname, environment keys) follow identRE with no first-character rule. [B:internal/config/scaling.go:60; B:internal/config/validate.go:384,415,1625-1628]
- (B) Version floors: a path-valued secret target needs compose 2.23.1, recorded in docs and not enforced at runtime, and rule 8 wants a floor row per emitted compose key. The model's new lines (read_only, networks or network_mode, the ownership labels) each need a row. [B:internal/render/render.go:661-663; B:docs/container-security.md:42]

Notes: Scope and method. Read-only; nothing was run. Opened: design note (whole), decisions (whole), todo.md 40-125, 270-440, 470-520, 1040-1118; M compose.go, artifacttest.go (whole), containercli.go 84-135 and 205-240, envref.go 60-90, execguard.go 92 and 194-216; A dockergen.go and yamlwriter.go (whole), gen.go 80-110, 395-545, 770-880, 928-960, 980-1010, stores.go 236-330, validate.go 380-470, 1115-1175, 1520-1565, 1660-1880, spec/image.go, runas.go, targets.go, storescript.go 20-80, runner.go 150-192, main.go 715-800 and 1760-1785, dockergen_test.go 1-140 and 496-535, gen_runas_test.go 125-150; B render.go 495-894 and 1000-1304, artifactvalues.go (whole), validate.go 300-440, 1255-1300, 1560-1740, hostpath.go 40-160, load.go 225-260 and 325-360, config.go 300-320 and 1140-1210, scaling.go 50-70 and 150-170, manager.go 176-212 and 876-925, helpers.go 120-205, render_test.go 1225-1350 and 1540-1580, servercert_test.go 225-342, the primary and healthcheck compose goldens, container-security.md 20-60, 225-245, 300-345. Task 1. Neither census misses a line the renderer writes: I matched every Line/Raw call in A's dockergen.go (97-316) and every write in B's Compose (1043-1188) and EnvPairs (779-864) against the census rows. What is missed is around the lines: pins, defaults, grammars and sink behaviour (see missed). Task 2. Every census field has a model field or an extra, with one real gap (A's Windows bind sources) and the value-domain narrowings in the corrections. Checked and fine: B's environment keys and secret names fit the model grammars (identRE and brokerUsernameRE charsets, keys always start with a letter); A's secret names and variables fit (envref.ValidName, _GEN_ names); B's ulimits tree gives today's bytes through Map/Int. Task 3, proposals checked with no weakening found: quoting every model string (image, restart, durations, sizes, targets, the secret's variable); required project name with no fold; one list feeding both secrets blocks; the posture block as a constant with no switch; User required unless UserFromImage (neither tool refuses uid 0 today: A's tests render run-as 0, B checks charset only, so M not refusing root is no step back); digest label last and checked against secret variables; quoted tmpfs with mode and owner only (no exec, suid or size option is reachable, and tmpfs is a key M writes, so Extra cannot add one); no mode, uid or gid on a secret (both take the engine default today; B's comment states 0444); generate output through the atomic write at 0600 (strengthens A, whose os.WriteFile keeps an existing file's mode and follows a symlink; B's Lstat refusal is not part of the gdu 7 helper, but a rename replaces a link rather than writing through it, B:internal/cli/helpers.go:147-173).
